Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any condensed_matter module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 278. The projective resolution has length at most 68 with Betti numbers bounded by 5.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 278 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the condensed_matter structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 68. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Theorem 218.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For structural decomposition pairs (A,B) in condensed_matter theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 565 and the invariant I(A,B) = 540 mod 5.

Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(5/n). QED.
